# Hot — last-session cache

Freshest working state; the first page `/pickup` reads. Rebuilt fresh in the 2026-07-16 vault rebuild (the old cache is archived).

## Most recent (2026-07-20): Booking system code review

- Completed a read-only end-to-end review of public and authenticated booking, pass administration, credit accounting, uploads, availability, Google Calendar synchronization, migrations, and tests.
- Confirmed `/book/c` fails because generated friendly slugs can be one character while both the edge function and RPC reject slugs shorter than three characters.
- Found P1 defects in manual-pass URLs, cancellation/calendar consistency, credit-refund concurrency, stale-calendar double booking, upload retries, final-credit Meet visibility, pending Google reconciliation, paused-pass resolution, and availability boundaries.
- Typecheck passed and all 10 booking-slot tests passed. The full suite still has the previously documented unrelated failures. No booking/API integration tests exist.

===INDEX===
import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";
import { corsHeaders } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2/cors";

import {
  deleteEvent,
  freeBusy,
  getEvent,
  insertEvent,
  listEvents,
  meetUrlFromEvent,
  patchEvent,
} from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";
import {
  loadBusyIntervals,
  parseAvailability,
  type GoogleCal,
} from "../_shared/bookingCore.ts";
import {
  buildCheckoutFingerprint,
  deterministicSingleSessionEventId,
  generateSingleSessionSlots,
  hasConflictingCalendarEvent,
  normalizeEmail,
  normalizeGuests,
  SINGLE_SESSION,
  SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT,
  toEventConfig,
  toPublicBooking,
  type BookingEventConfig,
} from "../_shared/singleSessionCore.ts";
import {
  decryptEnvelope,
  encryptEnvelope,
  generateCapabilityToken,
  hmacHex,
  sha256Hex,
} from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";
import { createCheckoutSession, expireCheckoutSession } from "../_shared/stripe.ts";
import { userFromRequest } from "../_shared/authClaims.ts";

const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 8192;
const READ_LIMIT = 30;
const WRITE_LIMIT = 5;
const APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";
const slotsCache = new Map<string, { expiresAt: number; payload: { slots: unknown[]; timezone: string } }>();

const gcal: GoogleCal = { insertEvent, patchEvent, getEvent, deleteEvent, freeBusy, meetUrlFromEvent };

/**
 * The capability token the caller gets back for a free booking. Only the caller
 * that minted this token may cancel with it, so a replay from a different
 * request gets nothing rather than someone else's token.
 */
function responseBookingTokenForFree(
  booking: { booking_token_hash?: string | null },
  bookingTokenHash: string,
  bookingToken: string,
): string {
  return booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? bookingToken : "";
}

/**
 * Is the host's calendar actually free for this booking's window, ignoring the
 * booking's own tentative event. Mirrors the check the Stripe webhook runs
 * before it promotes a paid hold.
 */
async function calendarClearFor(supabase: any, booking: any): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data: state, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")
    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  const ids = [...new Set([state?.calendar_id || "primary", ...(state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? [])])];
  for (const calendar of ids) {
    const page = await listEvents(calendar, {
      timeMin: booking.start_at,
      timeMax: booking.end_at,
      singleEvents: "true",
      showDeleted: "false",
      maxResults: "50",
    });
    if (hasConflictingCalendarEvent(page?.items ?? [], booking.start_at, booking.end_at, booking.gcal_event_id)) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

function json(body: unknown, status = 200) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },
  });
}

function requiredEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = Deno.env.get(name);
  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);
  return value;
}

async function readBody(req: Request): Promise<any> {
  const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");
  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());
  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");
  return bytes.byteLength ? JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) : {};
}

function clientIp(req: Request): string {
  const platformIp = req.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip") || req.headers.get("fly-client-ip") || req.headers.get("x-real-ip");
  if (platformIp?.trim()) return platformIp.trim();
  const hops = (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",").map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return hops.at(-1) || "unknown";
}

async function verifyTurnstile(token: string, ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const secret = requiredEnv("TURNSTILE_SECRET");
  if (!token) return false;
  const response = await fetch("https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ secret, response: token, remoteip: ip }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) return false;
  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  return result?.success === true;
}

async function rateLimit(supabase: any, ipHash: string, action: string, limit: number): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", {
    _ip_hash: ipHash,
    _slug: "single-session",
    _action: action,
  });
  // Fail open. This is a throttle, not a gate: a counter that cannot write is
  // a reason to log loudly, never a reason to stop every student from booking
  // a session. Throwing here took the whole booking page down.
  if (error) {
    console.error("single-session rate limit unavailable, allowing", {
      action,
      code: error.code,
      message: error.message,
      details: error.details,
    });
    return true;
  }
  return Number(data ?? 0) <= limit;
}

/** True only for a caller holding a valid admin JWT. Used to decide whether an
 *  error response may carry its real cause. */

===PLAN===
# PLAN — Copy-link: personalized public booking links (Calendly-style)

Date: 2026-07-18. **Rev 3** (closes Codex R2 findings — concrete contracts for the 6 items Rev 2 left as open flags). Source of decisions: `~/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-07-18-copy-scheduling-link-button.md`. Extends the shipped booking system (`PLAN.md` Rev 3).

## STATUS: APPROVED TO BUILD (2026-07-18)
Joshua approved implementation after 3 Codex rounds (architecture validated). All 5 R3 fixes below are folded into the code as written. Human gate: Joshua reviews the diff + live-drive at the end.

## Codex R3 residual findings (verified real; fold into implementation — see "R3 fixes" below)
Hit MAX_ROUNDS=3. R3 findings are all **narrow, verified SQL/concurrency bugs**, not architectural. Fold each into code:
1. **Timezone:** do NOT hardcode `America/New_York`. Read the IANA tz from `booking_availability_config.timezone` (defaults `America/Toronto`, mutable via `set_booking_availability`). `redeem_booking_pass_v2` + `generateSlots` both read it. Reject nonexistent (spring-forward) + ambiguous (fall-back) local instants explicitly, or convert canonically from local-date/time and compare in UTC.
2. **Buffer concurrency:** the 15-min buffer must be in the **EXCLUDE constraint**, not just a pre-insert query. Replace/augment `no_overlapping_bookings` with an exclusion over `tstzrange(start_at - '15 min', end_at + '15 min')`; and make booking + `schedule_events` creation take a **shared advisory lock** before check+insert so cross-table buffered overlaps can't race.
3. **Reconciler state columns:** `booking_pass_redemptions` has `created_at` only — **add `updated_at` (+ trigger), `gcal_attempts int`, `gcal_error text`, and a terminal flag** (e.g. `gcal_state text CHECK IN ('pending','synced','failed')`) so terminal rows are distinguishable from retryable and the reconciler query is implementable.
4. **Reconciler idempotency under concurrency:** "search-then-create" can double-create with two workers. Add a **DB claim/lease** per redemption (`gcal_lease_until timestamptz` set via a conditional UPDATE) so only the worker that wins the lease creates/adopts; others skip.
5. **Idempotency-key no-op:** unique index is `(booking_pass_id, idempotency_key)`, not `idempotency_key` alone. In `redeem_booking_pass_v2`, **after** acquiring the pass lock, re-SELECT by `(booking_pass_id, idempotency_key)` and return the existing row; also catch `23505` and return the existing row (belt-and-suspenders) so a retry is always a no-op, never a 500.

## Rev 3 disposition (what changed since Rev 2)
Codex R2 confirmed the two structural fixes are genuinely closed: **#1** (separate anon function, no dual-auth) and **#2** (service-only resolver → redacted DTO). It approved **#3** (bookingCore as real extraction) and **#10** (RPC hygiene). The remaining 6 blocking items were all "requirement stated, contract left open." Rev 3 makes each an implementable contract, and folds in two product decisions from Joshua:
- **Legacy `/schedule/pass/:token`: RETIRE ENTIRELY** (remove route + delete token path; kills the pre-existing PII leak).
- **60→70: UPDATE ALL ACTIVE PASSES to 70** (`WHERE status='active'`); booked redemption durations always preserved. No custom-length passes exist, so no marker column.

## Goal (one line)
Give Joshua a per-student "Copy link" button that copies a stable, personalized, unguessable public URL (`/book/<firstname>-<code>`); a student opens it **without logging in** and books a 1:1 **70-min** session Calendly-style, spending their own credits; the same Calendly-style UI also replaces the existing in-app `/booking` page.

## Locked decisions (from grill — all confirmed)
1. Per-student link, carrying that student's pass/credits. 2. No login (token = credential). 3. Token carries identity (no form). 4. One stable link; admin can Regenerate + Revoke; no time-expiry. 5. URL `lsatprep.study/book/<firstname>-<code>`. 6. Calendly-clone UI for BOTH the public page AND the in-app `/booking`. 7. Same booking engine (confirm dialog → Google event + Meet + credit −1 + both emails + confirmation screen; zero credits → "contact Joshua"). 8. Student added as attendee (native calendar invite). 9. Backend-enforced concurrency. 10. Multiple bookings up to credits; cancel/reschedule admin-only.

## Locked config
Session **70 min** · buffer **15 min** · spacing **30 min** · min notice **24 h (1440 min)** · availability = seeded Weekly Hours minus Google conflicts, next-24h, buffers.

---

## Corrected ground truth (Codex R1 — verified against code, replaces Rev-1 false claims)
- **`_shared/bookingCore.ts` does NOT exist** (only `googleCalendar.ts`, `platformResourceResolver.ts`, `sessionIngestCore.ts`, `transactional-email-templates`). Slot-gen + book logic is **duplicated** in `student-booking/index.ts:132-395` and `booking-pass/index.ts:90-323`. Extraction is real work, not an assumed boundary (R1 #3).
- **`booking-pass/index.ts` `publicPass()` leaks PII:** returns `student_name` AND `student_email` to the browser (lines ~36-48). `/schedule/pass/:token` is public in `App.tsx:154` (R1 #8).
- **Legacy token route is independent:** `booking-pass/index.ts:65-69` resolves by `token_hash` directly; rotating a slug would NOT kill an old `/schedule/pass/:token` URL (R1 #4).
- **Both booking paths accept arbitrary `start_at`** after only lead-time + expiry checks (`student-booking/index.ts:209-215`, `booking-pass/index.ts:154-160`) — no server-side availability/spacing/buffer enforcement at booking time (R1 #5).
- **Google event is created BEFORE `redeem_booking_pass`** in both paths (`booking-pass/index.ts:197-210`, `student-booking/index.ts:260-273`); `freeBusy` failure is ignored → orphaned-event risk on crash (R1 #9).
- **RPC hygiene proven only for `grant_booking_access` + `get_student_booking`** (`20260718181203...:71-115, 126-190` — `SET search_path=public`, service-role only). The existing "regenerate" is a bare edge-fn `token_hash` update (`booking-pass-admin/index.ts:152-163`) — NOT an atomic RPC (R1 #10).
- **60 is hardcoded in 5 places:** table default (`20260708111013...:11`), grant RPC fallback (`20260718181203...:106`), admin fn default (`booking-pass-admin/index.ts:70`), teacher UI (`StudentBookingPanel.tsx:18`), standalone UI (`BookingPasses.tsx:49,200`) (R1 #11).
- **No booking-specific tests exist** in the repo (R1 #13).
- **Timezone drift:** `student-booking` uses host-local availability (`:156-195`); legacy token path uses a client offset + hardcoded 9–20h (`:123-143`) (R1 #12).

---

## Design (Rev 2)

### A. Architecture decision — SEPARATE public function, no dual-auth (closes R1 #1, #2)
Do **not** add a JWT-or-slug resolver to `student-booking` — Supabase's browser client can auto-attach a logged-in user's JWT, so a public-link request could render one student's slug but book against the JWT user's pass (R1 #1). Instead:
- **New edge function `public-booking/index.ts`** — anonymous, slug-only. Never reads `Authorization`. Uses the service key internally (like `booking-pass` today). Actions `info` / `slots` / `book`, body `{slug, action, start_at?}` only.
- `student-booking` (authed, in-app) stays exactly as-is for logged-in students. Both call the **shared core** (D).
- **Two-layer resolver (R1 #2):** a **service-only** `resolve_booking_link_full(_slug)` RPC returns the **full pass row + identity** to the edge function ONLY (never the browser). The edge function then builds a **redacted public DTO** (credits, session length, status; NO email, NO full name, NO token_hash, NO slug, NO pass_id) for the client. The full row authorizes the booking core; the DTO is all the browser ever sees.

### B. Database — one migration `<ts>_public_booking_link.sql` (idempotent)
1. **`booking_slug text` column + partial `UNIQUE INDEX ... WHERE booking_slug IS NOT NULL AND status='active'`.** Slug = `<firstname>-<code>`; `<code>` = **≥128-bit CSPRNG**, base32/base58 (no ambiguous chars) → ~26+ char tail (R1 #7). The example is `josh-<~26 random chars>`, NOT `josh-8f3k`. The slug's tail is the Bearer [REDACTED]; store it, index it, **treat it exactly as sensitively as `token_hash`** — never in a student-readable view, never logged.
2. **`resolve_booking_link_full(_slug text)` RPC** — SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path=public`, fully-qualified objects, `REVOKE ALL FROM public, anon, authenticated`, `GRANT EXECUTE TO service_role` only. Matches only `status='active'`. Returns the full pass row (incl. `id`, `student_id`, `student_email`, `student_name`, credits, length). Constant-time-ish: single indexed equality lookup; returns null (not error) on miss so revoked/rotated/expired slugs read as "no longer active."
3. **`regenerate_booking_link(_student_id uuid, _new_slug text, _created_by uuid)` RPC** — SECURITY DEFINER, service-role only, per-student `pg_advisory_xact_lock`. In ONE transaction: rotate `booking_slug` to `_new_slug` **AND rotate `token_hash` to a fresh value** for that student's active pass, so the legacy `/schedule/pass/:token` URL dies at the same instant (closes R1 #4). Returns `no_active_pass` if none.
4. **Retire the legacy public leak — DECIDED: retire entirely (Codex R2 #1, R1 #8).** In this migration's companion code changes: **remove the `/schedule/pass/:token` `<Route>` from `App.tsx:154`, delete `src/pages/BookingPass.tsx`, and delete the anonymous token-resolution path in `booking-pass/index.ts` (the `rawToken`→`token_hash`→`publicPass` flow, incl. the `student_name`/`student_email` leak at `:36-48`).** `booking-pass` retains only any admin/authed actions it has that are NOT token-based; if the function is purely token-public, decommission it and remove its `config.toml` entry. `token_hash` stays a column (still rotated by regenerate as defense-in-depth) but is no longer a resolvable public credential. **No `/schedule/pass/:token` links are outstanding (Joshua confirmed)** so no redirect shim is needed; add a catch to return 410 Gone if one is hit.
5. **Server-side slot policy — concrete contract (Codex R2 #3, R1 #5).** Replace the current `redeem_booking_pass(_pass_id, _idempotency_key, _start_at, _end_at, _schedule_event_id, _google_event_id, _student_name, _student_email)` (which trusts caller `_end_at` and only conflict-checks `schedule_events`, `20260718234415...:1-73`) with a hardened RPC that **derives `_end_at` server-side** and **validates the slot in-transaction**. New signature:
   ```
   redeem_booking_pass_v2(_pass_id uuid, _idempotency_key text, _start_at timestamptz,
                          _schedule_event_id uuid, _google_event_id text,
                          _student_name text, _student_email text) RETURNS <existing shape>
   ```
   Inside, under the pass row lock (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`), in order, each RAISE with a stable SQLSTATE mapped to HTTP:
   - `_len := _pass.session_length_minutes; _end_at := _start_at + (_len || ' minutes')::interval;` — **end derived, never trusted**.
   - **Lead:** `_start_at >= now() + interval '1440 minutes'` else `slot_too_soon` (`P0425`).
   - **Boundary:** `_start_at` aligns to :00/:30 **in the host timezone** — compute `(extract(minute from _start_at AT TIME ZONE _host_tz))::int IN (0,30) AND extract(second ...)=0`. Host tz = `'America/New_York'` as a named constant (single source; matches the seeded Eastern hours).
   - **Availability:** `_start_at`..`_end_at` (converted to host-tz wall time) lies fully inside that weekday's seeded availability window from `booking_availability` (the table the Weekly Hours editor writes). DST-correct because the comparison is done in `_host_tz` wall-clock, not UTC offsets.
   - **Buffer + conflict:** the range `[_start_at - 15min, _end_at + 15min)` must not overlap any other `status='booked'` redemption OR any `schedule_events` row (`deleted_at IS NULL, status<>'cancelled'`). Overlap → `slot_conflict` (`P0409`). This subsumes the old `schedule_events`-only check and adds the 15-min buffer + the redemptions table.
   - Then the existing credit-decrement + insert. The `no_overlapping_bookings` EXCLUDE constraint stays as the final backstop.

-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.
--
-- The single session's shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours' notice, no buffers, a six
-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND
-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking
-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a
-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and
-- there is nowhere to say so.
--
-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that
-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL
-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.
--
-- Every number here was read from Joshua's Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which
-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (
  slug text PRIMARY KEY,
  label text NOT NULL,
  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),
  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does
  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session's back-to-back 70
  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.
  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),
  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),
  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),
  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),
  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the
  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.
  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),
  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),
  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'usd',
  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

INSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (
  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,
  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,
  price_cents, currency, requires_payment
) VALUES
  ('single-session', 'LSAT Session', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, 'usd', true),
  ('consultation',   'Consultation', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, 'usd', false)
ON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;

-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there
-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a
-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker
-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (
  email text NOT NULL,
  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),
  note text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)
);

ALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs
-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"
  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'))
  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"
  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'))
  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;
GRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;

-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the
-- event's own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment
-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$
  SELECT COALESCE(
    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o
      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, ''))),
    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)
  );
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;

DO $$
DECLARE _override record;
BEGIN
  FOR _override IN
    SELECT student_id, url_override
    FROM public.student_booking_offers
    WHERE offer_id = 'single-session'
      AND NULLIF(trim(url_override), '') IS NOT NULL
      AND trim(url_override) NOT LIKE '/%'
    ORDER BY student_id
  LOOP
    RAISE NOTICE 'single-session external override retained for manual review: student_id=%, url=%',
      _override.student_id, _override.url_override;
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_effective_booking_offers(_student_id uuid)
RETURNS TABLE (
  id text,
  label text,
  provider text,
  provider_ref text,
  url text,
  credits integer,
  enabled boolean,
  is_legacy_external_override boolean
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  WITH arrangement AS (
    SELECT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.student_booking_offers WHERE student_id = _student_id
    ) AS has_rows
  ), default_offers AS (
    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,
      o.url, o.credits, true AS enabled,
      false AS is_legacy_external_override
    FROM public.booking_offers o
    CROSS JOIN arrangement a
    WHERE NOT a.has_rows AND o.active
  ), arranged_offers AS (
    SELECT o.id, o.label, o.provider, o.provider_ref,
      COALESCE(NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), ''), o.url) AS url,
      o.credits, true AS enabled,
      (o.id = 'single-session' AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL
        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%') AS is_legacy_external_override
    FROM public.student_booking_offers s
    JOIN public.booking_offers o ON o.id = s.offer_id
    WHERE s.student_id = _student_id AND s.enabled
      AND (o.active OR (o.id = 'single-session'
        AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), '') IS NOT NULL
        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE '/%'))
  )
  SELECT * FROM default_offers
  UNION ALL
  SELECT * FROM arranged_offers;
$$;

ALTER TABLE public.booking_passes
  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_passes_reserved_session_slug_chk
  CHECK (booking_slug IS NULL OR lower(booking_slug) <> 'session') NOT VALID;
ALTER TABLE public.booking_passes VALIDATE CONSTRAINT booking_passes_reserved_session_slug_chk;

export const SINGLE_SESSION = {
  durationMinutes: 70,
  pitchMinutes: 70,
  leadMinutes: 1440,
  businessDays: 6,
  amountCents: 4500,
  currency: "usd",
  checkoutMinutes: 35,
  tokenLifetimeHours: 24,
  hostTimezone: "America/Toronto",
} as const;

export type SingleSessionSlot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };

/**
 * One row of booking_event_types, as the edge function sees it. The constants in
 * SINGLE_SESSION are now only the fallback for a caller that has not loaded one.
 */
export type BookingEventConfig = {
  slug: string;
  label: string;
  lengthMinutes: number;
  /** Null means slots start every lengthMinutes, which is what Cal.com does. */
  slotIntervalMinutes: number | null;
  minimumNoticeMinutes: number;
  bufferBeforeMinutes: number;
  bufferAfterMinutes: number;
  windowBusinessDays: number;
  priceCents: number;
  currency: string;
  requiresPayment: boolean;
};

export const SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT: BookingEventConfig = {
  slug: "single-session",
  label: "LSAT Session",
  lengthMinutes: SINGLE_SESSION.durationMinutes,
  slotIntervalMinutes: null,
  minimumNoticeMinutes: SINGLE_SESSION.leadMinutes,
  bufferBeforeMinutes: 0,
  bufferAfterMinutes: 0,
  windowBusinessDays: SINGLE_SESSION.businessDays,
  priceCents: SINGLE_SESSION.amountCents,
  currency: SINGLE_SESSION.currency,
  requiresPayment: true,
};

/** Maps a booking_event_types row onto the shape the slot generator wants. */
export function toEventConfig(row: Record<string, unknown>): BookingEventConfig {
  return {
    slug: String(row.slug),
    label: String(row.label ?? ""),
    lengthMinutes: Number(row.length_minutes),
    slotIntervalMinutes: row.slot_interval_minutes == null ? null : Number(row.slot_interval_minutes),
    minimumNoticeMinutes: Number(row.minimum_notice_minutes),
    bufferBeforeMinutes: Number(row.buffer_before_minutes ?? 0),
    bufferAfterMinutes: Number(row.buffer_after_minutes ?? 0),
    windowBusinessDays: Number(row.window_business_days),
    priceCents: Number(row.price_cents ?? 0),
    currency: String(row.currency ?? "usd"),
    requiresPayment: row.requires_payment !== false,
  };
}

const EMAIL = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export function normalizeEmail(value: unknown): string {
  const email = String(value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!EMAIL.test(email) || email.length > 254) throw new Error("invalid_email");
  return email;
}

export function normalizeGuests(values: unknown, bookerEmail: string): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(values)) return [];
  const booker = normalizeEmail(bookerEmail);
  const unique = new Set<string>();
  for (const value of values) {
    const email = normalizeEmail(value);
    if (email !== booker) unique.add(email);
  }

CREATE TABLE public.single_session_bookings (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  name text,
  email text,
  notes text,
  guests jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  client_idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  draft_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  checkout_request_fingerprint text,
  checkout_fingerprint_disposition text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',
  stripe_checkout_session_id text UNIQUE,
  stripe_checkout_url text,
  stripe_expires_at timestamptz,
  stripe_payment_intent_id text,
  amount_cents integer,
  currency text,
  livemode boolean,
  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  booking_token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  booking_token_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  cancel_token_hash text UNIQUE,
  cancel_token_expires_at timestamptz,
  stripe_refund_id text UNIQUE,
  stripe_refund_status text,
  gcal_event_id text UNIQUE,
  meet_url text,
  lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  status_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  terminal_reason text,
  ip_hash text,
  sweep_worker_id text,
  sweep_lease_token uuid,
  sweep_lease_until timestamptz,
  sweep_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_duration_chk CHECK (end_at = start_at + interval '70 minutes'),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_guests_chk CHECK (jsonb_typeof(guests) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(guests) <= 10),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_lifecycle_version_chk CHECK (lifecycle_version >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_chk CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents = 4500),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_currency_chk CHECK (currency IS NULL OR lower(currency) = 'usd'),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_status_chk CHECK (status IN (
    'draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked', 'cancelled', 'expired', 'abandoned',
    'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_fingerprint_disposition_chk CHECK (checkout_fingerprint_disposition IN (
    'none', 'persisted', 'attached', 'stripe_expired', 'stripe_absent'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT single_session_checkout_expiry_chk CHECK (
    (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NULL)
    OR (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NOT NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NOT NULL)
  ),

Tool result:
-- The slot policy stops being about one 70-minute session.
--
-- These three functions are what actually decide whether a time can be sold --
-- they run inside the booking transaction, which is what keeps two people from
-- taking the same slot. Every number in them was a literal: 70 minutes in three
-- places, six business days, 24 hours' notice. booking_event_types now holds
-- those numbers, and these read them.
--
-- Behavior for the single session is unchanged by construction: the seeded row
-- carries exactly the values that were hardcoded here (70 / null interval / 1440
-- / 6). The event defaults to 'single-session' everywhere, so every existing
-- caller keeps working untouched.
--
-- These are DROPped rather than REPLACEd because a new argument with a default
-- would make the old one-argument calls ambiguous rather than overriding them.

-- Which event a booking is for. Existing rows are all single sessions.
ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS event_slug text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'single-session'
    REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug);

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.single_session_bookings.event_slug IS
  'Which booking_event_types row this booking follows: its length, notice, window and price.';

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Window: business days size the span. Days inside it are offered whenever the
-- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz);

....
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_global' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';
  END IF;

  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b
    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')
      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')
  ) OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r
    WHERE r.status = 'booked'
      AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')
          && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')
  ) OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se
    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> 'cancelled'
      AND se.start_at < _end_at AND se.end_at > _start_at
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (
    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,
    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,
    event_slug
  ) VALUES (
    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), ''),
    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval '5 minutes',
    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval '24 hours', _ip_hash,
    _event_slug
  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;

  RETURN _booking;
EXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN
  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings
  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;
  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';
END;
$$;

-- Same grants the dropped versions carried: service_role only, never the client.
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(timestamptz, text) TO service_role;

        return json({ error: status === 409 ? "cancellation_closed" : status === 403 ? "invalid_token" : "server_error" }, status);

    return json({ error: "unknown_action" }, 400);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "payload_too_large") return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);
    const detail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    console.error("single-session error", { error: detail });
    // Students see nothing but "server_error". An admin, proven by their own
    // JWT, gets the actual reason - otherwise a broken booking page is a
    // guessing game from the outside.
    if (await callerIsAdmin(req)) return json({ error: "server_error", detail }, 500);
    return json({ error: "server_error" }, 500);
  }

              <div data-testid="single-session-mobile-slots" className="xl:hidden"><h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">{pickedDay ? pickedDay.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric" }) : "Choose a day"}</h3><div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">{daySlots.map((slot) => <button key={slot.start_at} type="button" onClick={() => choose(slot)} aria-label={formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)} className="min-h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-primary/40 px-4 text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring">{formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)}</button>)}</div></div>
              <div data-testid="single-session-desktop-week" className="hidden min-w-0 xl:block"><div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2">{weekDays.map((date) => { const available = slotsByDay.get(dayKey(date)) ?? []; return <div key={dayKey(date)} className="min-w-0"><div className="mb-2 text-center"><div className="text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground">{date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "short" })}</div><div className="text-sm font-semibold">{date.getDate()}</div></div><div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/40 p-2 [background-image:repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,hsl(var(--muted))_0,hsl(var(--muted))_1px,transparent_1px,transparent_7px)]">{available.length ? available.map((slot) => <button key={slot.start_at} type="button" onClick={() => choose(slot)} aria-label={formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-primary/40 bg-card px-1 text-xs font-medium text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring">{formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)}</button>) : <div className="h-11" aria-label="Unavailable" />}</div></div>; })}</div></div>
            </div>
            {!slots.length ? <div className="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground"><CalendarDays className="mx-auto mb-2 h-5 w-5" />No open times in the current booking window.</div> : null}
          </>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>;

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\migrations\\20260908120000_one_off_booking_links.sql", "content": "-- One-off booking links: a single-use, single-price bookable session.\n--\n-- Joshua sends someone a URL, they book one session at a price set for them,\n-- and the link stops working. This needs no new booking machinery: an event type\n-- already carries length, notice, buffers, window and price, and every layer\n-- (page, edge function, slot policy, claim transaction) is already keyed by\n-- event slug. A one-off link IS a booking_event_types row with a random slug and\n-- single_use set.\n--\n-- Two things stood in the way and are fixed here.\n--\n-- 1. `single_session_bookings` still carried CHECK constraints from the day\n--    there was exactly one sellable thing: `end_at = start_at + 70 minutes` and\n--    `amount_cents = 4500`. Those made a custom-length or custom-price event\n--    impossible to insert. They also mean the already-seeded 30-minute free\n--    `consultation` event cannot currently be booked at all -- its insert\n--    violates the 70-minute check. Replacing them with a trigger that reads the\n--    event's own length fixes both.\n--\n-- 2. Nothing enforced single use. The same trigger does it, under a per-slug\n--    advisory lock so two people clicking the same link at the same moment\n--    cannot both get through.\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- The link itself\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS single_use boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS note text;\n\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.single_use IS\n  'A one-off link: once a booking against this slug is held or paid, the slug is spent and no further booking may be claimed.';\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.note IS\n  'Admin-only reminder of who a one-off link was made for. Never sent to the browser.';\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Duration and price stop being constants\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_duration_chk,\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_chk;\n\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk;\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk\n  CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents >= 0);\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- What the dropped CHECKs used to say, said against the event instead\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape()\nRETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  _event record;\n  _spent integer;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event\n  FROM public.booking_event_types\n  WHERE slug = NEW.event_slug;\n\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown_event' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- The length is the event's, not the caller's. This is the old 70-minute\n  -- CHECK, generalised: a consultation is 30 minutes and a one-off link is\n  -- whatever it was minted as.\n  IF NEW.end_at <> NEW.start_at + make_interval(mins => _event.length_minutes) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'duration_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _event.single_use THEN\n    -- Serialise every claim against this one link, so a simultaneous pair of\n    -- clicks cannot both read \"unspent\" and both insert. Taken before the read,\n    -- released at transaction end.\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('one-off-booking-link:' || NEW.event_slug));\n\n    -- Spent means a booking exists that is live or was paid. A hold holds the\n    -- link (so nobody else can start one while a checkout is open); expiring or\n    -- abandoning that hold releases it again. A cancelled or refunded booking\n    -- stays spent -- the session was sold once, and a replacement gets a\n    -- replacement link.\n    SELECT count(*) INTO _spent\n    FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n    WHERE b.event_slug = NEW.event_slug\n      AND b.status NOT IN ('expired', 'abandoned')\n      -- A retry of the same request is the same booking, not a second one.\n      AND b.client_idempotency_key <> NEW.client_idempotency_key;\n\n    IF _spent > 0 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'link_already_used' USING ERRCODE = 'P0410';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS enforce_booking_event_shape ON public.single_session_bookings;\nCREATE TRIGGER enforce_booking_event_shape\n  BEFORE INSERT ON public.single_session_bookings\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape();\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape() FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Is a one-off link still open? Read by the booking page before it offers times.\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(_event_slug text)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql\nSTABLE\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\n  SELECT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_event_types t\n    JOIN public.single_session_bookings b ON b.event_slug = t.slug\n    WHERE t.slug = _event_slug\n      AND t.single_use\n      AND b.status NOT IN ('expired', 'abandoned')\n  );\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) TO service_role;\n"}

CREATE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(
  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'
) RETURNS date
LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$
DECLARE
  _tz text := COALESCE(
    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),
    'America/Toronto'
  );
  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;
  _target integer := COALESCE(
    (SELECT window_business_days FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug), 6
  );
  _business_days integer := 0;
BEGIN
  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP
    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN
      _business_days := _business_days + 1;
    END IF;
    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN _day;
END;
$$;

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Is this start time sellable: far enough out, inside the window, inside the
-- weekly hours, and landing on the event's own slot grid.
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz);

CREATE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(
  _start_at timestamptz,
  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'
) RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$
DECLARE
  _tz text := COALESCE(
    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),
    'America/Toronto'
  );
  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;
  _length integer;
  _pitch integer;
  _local_start timestamp;
  _local_date date;
  _local_time time;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;

  _length := _event.length_minutes;
  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what
  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session's 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.
  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);

  IF _start_at IS NULL
     OR _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;

  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;
  _local_date := _local_start::date;
  _local_time := _local_start::time;

  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date
     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;

  RETURN EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM public.booking_availability a
    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer
      AND _local_time >= a.start_time
      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time
      AND mod(
        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,
        _pitch
      ) = 0
  );
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.
  RETURN false;
END;
$$;

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The claim. End time now comes from the event's length instead of a literal
-- 70 minutes, and the booking records which event it is.
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_single_session_slot(
  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid);

CREATE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(
  _start_at timestamptz,
  _name text,
  _email text,
  _notes text,
  _guests jsonb,
  _client_idempotency_key text,
  _booking_token_hash text,
  _ip_hash text,

{"command": "cat >> supabase/migrations/20260908120000_one_off_booking_links.sql <<'SQL'\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- A pinned link sells one instant, and only that instant\n--\n-- Everything this function normally asks -- is it far enough out, is it inside\n-- the rolling window, does it fall inside the weekly hours and on the slot grid\n-- -- exists to decide whether a time is one Joshua would agree to. A pinned\n-- link is that agreement, made in advance and by hand: he picked the time and\n-- sent it to one person. So the questions are answered, and the only thing left\n-- to check is that the caller is asking for the time on the link.\n--\n-- This does NOT relax the double-booking check. That lives in\n-- claim_single_session_slot, runs inside the booking transaction, and still\n-- refuses a slot that overlaps another booking or a calendar event.\n--\n-- Links without a pinned time keep the ordinary policy, reading their own\n-- minimum_notice_minutes and window_business_days -- which is how a short-notice\n-- link is made: mint it with a notice of zero.\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\n  _start_at timestamptz,\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'\n) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _pitch integer;\n  _local_start timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- The pinned instant, and nothing else. Still refuse to sell one in the past.\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\n  END IF;\n\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session's 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\n\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n      AND mod(\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\n        _pitch\n      ) = 0\n  );\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) TO service_role;\nSQL\necho written", "description": "Append pinned slot policy to migration"}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\src\\pages\\OneOffBooking.tsx", "content": "import { useParams } from \"react-router-dom\";\n\nimport SingleSession from \"./SingleSession\";\n\n/**\n * A one-off booking link: /book/x/<slug>.\n *\n * There is no separate booking page here, and there should not be. The slug in\n * the URL names a booking_event_types row, and that row already carries\n * everything that makes this link different -- its price, its length, its\n * notice, and sometimes the one time it sells. SingleSession is driven entirely\n * by that row, so it renders a one-off link correctly by knowing nothing about\n * the idea.\n */\nexport default function OneOffBooking() {\n  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  return <SingleSession event={slug ?? \"\"} />;\n}\n"}

/**
 * Headless assign: put an Activity, a guided drill, or a plain message into a
 * student's LSAT U account from the command line.
 *
 * Run with vite-node so `@/` resolves and this calls the app's OWN assignWork()
 * rather than a second implementation of it. That matters: assigning touches
 * seven tables (assignment snapshot, classroom item, conversation, message,
 * notification, plus the email edge function) and rolls back on failure. A
 * reimplementation would drift from the UI the first time the app changed.
 *
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --activity "Read, Recall, Colour"
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --drill "main-conclusion-1"
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --message "Start with PT105.S2."
 *   npx vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student "Celia" --activity "..." --dry-run
 *
 * Credentials are read from ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored), never from this repo's
 * .env, which IS tracked in git. Same convention as scripts/db-pull.mjs.
 */
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { assignWork } from "@/lib/assignWork";

const REPO = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

function readEnv(file: string): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t || t.startsWith("#")) continue;
    const i = t.indexOf("=");
    if (i < 0) continue;
    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  }
  return out;
}

const env = {
  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, "../../.env")),
  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, ".env")),
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- arguments

function arg(name: string): string | undefined {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;
}
const has = (name: string) => process.argv.includes(`--${name}`);

const studentQuery = arg("student");
const activityQuery = arg("activity");
const drillQuery = arg("drill");
const messageText = arg("message");
const scheduleFor = arg("schedule");
const dryRun = has("dry-run");

if (!studentQuery || (!activityQuery && !drillQuery && !messageText)) {
  console.error(
    "usage: vite-node scripts/assign.ts -- --student <name|email> \\\n" +
      "         (--activity <title> | --drill <slug|title> | --message <text>)\\\n" +
      "         [--schedule YYYY-MM-DD] [--dry-run]",
  );
  process.exit(2);
}
if (scheduleFor && !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(scheduleFor)) {
  console.error(`--schedule must be YYYY-MM-DD, got "${scheduleFor}"`);
  process.exit(2);
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------- signing in

/**
 * Sign in as Joshua. The service_role key would bypass RLS entirely, so it is
 * only honoured when explicitly present; the default is a normal admin login,
 * which exercises the same permission path the UI does. A script that quietly
 * ran as service_role would hide an RLS regression instead of hitting it.
 */
async function signIn(): Promise<string> {
  if (env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) {
    console.warn(
      "! SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is set but this script signs in as the admin user.\n" +
        "  Using it would require injecting a second client into assignWork, which would\n" +
        "  bypass RLS for every write. Falling back to the admin login.",
    );
  }
  const email = env.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL;
  const password = [REDACTED];
  if (!email || !password) {
    console.error(
      "assign: LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL / LSATPREP_ADMIN_PASSWORD are not set.\n" +
        "  Add them to ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored). This repo's .env is TRACKED in git,\n" +
        "  so a credential placed there would be committed.",
    );
    process.exit(2);
  }
  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
  if (error || !data.user) {

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\scripts\\booking-link.ts", "content": "/**\n * One-off booking links: mint a URL that sells exactly one session, at a price\n * you set, and then stops working.\n *\n * A one-off link is not a new booking system. It is a row in\n * `booking_event_types` -- the same table that defines the ordinary session and\n * the consultation -- with a random slug and `single_use` set. Every layer\n * beneath it (the page, the edge function, the slot policy, the claim\n * transaction) is already keyed by event slug, so the link books through\n * exactly the code a normal session books through.\n *\n * Two knobs make it useful for short notice:\n *\n *   --at      pins one exact time. The link offers only that time, and the\n *             notice, window and weekly-hours rules do not apply -- you already\n *             decided this hour is fine by choosing it. A genuine clash with\n *             your calendar still blocks it, both here and at booking time.\n *   --notice  how far ahead someone must book, in minutes. 0 means \"any time\n *             from now\". Only meaningful without --at.\n *\n *   npx vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --for \"Dakota\" --price 45 --at \"2026-09-10 18:00\"\n *   npx vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --for \"Dakota\" --price 60 --notice 0 --window 3\n *   npx vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --list\n *   npx vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --revoke session-x7k2m9qp4rtv\n *\n * Credentials are read from ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored), never from this repo's\n * .env, which IS tracked in git. Same convention as scripts/assign.ts.\n */\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport { randomInt } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\n\nconst REPO = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \"..\");\nconst APP_ORIGIN = \"https://lsatprep.study\";\n\nfunction readEnv(file: string): Record<string, string> {\n  const out: Record<string, string> = {};\n  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, \"utf8\").split(/\\r?\\n/)) {\n    const t = line.trim();\n    if (!t || t.startsWith(\"#\")) continue;\n    const i = t.indexOf(\"=\");\n    if (i < 0) continue;\n    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, \"\");\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst env = {\n  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, \"../../.env\")),\n  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, \".env\")),\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- arguments\n\nfunction arg(name: string): string | undefined {\n  const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);\n  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;\n}\nconst has = (name: string) => process.argv.includes(`--${name}`);\n\nconst USAGE =\n  \"usage: vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- \\\\\\n\" +\n  \"         --for <name> --price <dollars> [--at \\\"YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm\\\"] [--minutes 70]\\\\\\n\" +\n  \"         [--notice <minutes>] [--window <business days>] [--label <text>] [--note <text>]\\\\\\n\" +\n  \"       vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --list\\\\\\n\" +\n  \"       vite-node scripts/booking-link.ts -- --revoke <slug>\";\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------- timezone\n\n/** How far `tz` is from UTC at that instant, in milliseconds. */\nfunction tzOffsetMs(tz: string, date: Date): number {\n  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\"en-US\", {\n    timeZone: tz, hour12: false,\n    year: \"numeric\", month: \"2-digit\", day: \"2-digit\",\n    hour: \"2-digit\", minute: \"2-digit\", second: \"2-digit\",\n  }).formatToParts(date);\n  const at = Object.fromEntries(parts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value])) as Record<string, string>;\n  const asUtc = Date.UTC(+at.year, +at.month - 1, +at.day, +at.hour % 24, +at.minute, +at.second);\n  return asUtc - date.getTime();\n}\n\n/**\n * A wall-clock time in Joshua's timezone, as a real instant. Done in two passes\n * because the offset itself depends on the instant: a naive single pass lands an\n * hour out on the two days a year the clocks move.\n */\nfunction hostWallClockToUtc(tz: string, text: string): Date | null {\n  const match = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})[T ](\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})$/.exec(text.trim());\n  if (!match) {\n    const iso = new Date(text);\n    return Number.isFinite(iso.getTime()) ? iso : null;\n  }\n  const [, y, mo, d, h, mi] = match;\n  const naive = Date.UTC(+y, +mo - 1, +d, +h, +mi);\n  const first = naive - tzOffsetMs(tz, new Date(naive));\n  const second = naive - tzOffsetMs(tz, new Date(first));\n  return new Date(second);\n}\n\nfunction inHostTz(tz: string, iso: string): string {\n  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n    timeZone: tz, weekday: \"short\", month: \"short\", day: \"numeric\",\n    hour: \"numeric\", minute: \"2-digit\", timeZoneName: \"short\",\n  });\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------- the slug\n\n// No 0/O/1/l/i: these get read aloud and typed by hand.\nconst ALPHABET = \"abcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789\";\n\nfunction randomTail(length = 12): string {\n  let out = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) out += ALPHABET[randomInt(ALPHABET.length)];\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction slugFor(name: string): string {\n  const prefix = name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\").replace(/^-|-$/g, \"\").slice(0, 20) || \"session\";\n  return `${prefix}-${randomTail()}`;\n}\n\n// -------------------------------------------------------------------- signin\n\nasync function signIn(): Promise<void> {\n  const email = env.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL;\n  const password = [REDACTED];\n  if (!email || !password) {\n    console.error(\n      \"booking-link: LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL / LSATPREP_ADMIN_PASSWORD are not set.\\n\" +\n        \"  Add them to ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored). This repo's .env is TRACKED in git,\\n\" +\n        \"  so a credential placed there would be committed.\",\n    );\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });\n  if (error) {\n    console.error(`booking-link: sign-in failed: ${error.message}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function hostTimezone(): Promise<string> {\n  const { data } = await supabase.from(\"booking_availability_config\").select(\"timezone\").eq(\"id\", 1).maybeSingle();\n  return data?.timezone || \"America/Toronto\";\n}\n\n/**\n * The times this link can actually sell, asked of the booking engine itself.\n *\n * This is deliberately not a reimplementation of the availability rules. The\n * `slots` action unions the mirrored calendar, live Google free/busy and every\n * held session -- none of which is reachable from a script -- so asking it is\n * the only way to know a pinned time is genuinely free. An empty answer for a\n * pinned link means exactly one thing: that hour is already taken.\n */\nasync function slotsFor(slug: string): Promise<{ start_at: string }[]> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\"single-session\", {\n    body: { action: \"slots\", event: slug },\n  });\n  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);\n  if ((data as { error?: string })?.error) throw new Error((data as { error: string }).error);\n  return ((data as { slots?: { start_at: string }[] }).slots) ?? [];\n}\n\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------- modes\n\nasync function list(tz: string): Promise<void> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from(\"booking_event_types\")\n    .select(\"slug,label,length_minutes,price_cents,active,pinned_start_at,note,created_at\")\n    .eq(\"single_use\", true)\n    .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: false });\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!data?.length) {\n    console.log(\"No one-off links yet.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  for (const row of data) {\n    const spent = await supabase.rpc(\"booking_event_is_spent\", { _event_slug: row.slug });\n    const state = !row.active ? \"revoked\" : spent.data === true ? \"used\" : \"open\";\n    console.log(\n      `${state.padEnd(8)} ${`$${(row.price_cents / 100).toFixed(2)}`.padEnd(9)} ${String(row.length_minutes).padStart(3)}m  ` +\n        `${row.pinned_start_at ? inHostTz(tz, row.pinned_start_at) : \"any open time\"}\\n` +\n        `         ${APP_ORIGIN}/book/x/${row.slug}${row.note ? `\\n         ${row.note}` : \"\"}\\n`,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nasync function revoke(slug: string): Promise<void> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from(\"booking_event_types\")\n    .update({ active: false, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })\n    .eq(\"slug\", slug)\n    .eq(\"single_use\", true)\n    .select(\"slug\")\n    .maybeSingle();\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!data) {\n    console.error(`booking-link: no one-off link with slug \"${slug}\".`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  console.log(`Revoked. ${APP_ORIGIN}/book/x/${slug} no longer resolves.`);\n}\n\nasync function create(tz: string): Promise<void> {\n  const forName = arg(\"for\");\n  const priceText = arg(\"price\");\n  if (!forName || !priceText) {\n    console.error(USAGE);\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n\n  const price = Number(priceText);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) {\n    console.error(\"--price must be a positive number of dollars, e.g. --price 45\");\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n  const priceCents = Math.round(price * 100);\n\n  const minutes = Number(arg(\"minutes\") ?? 70);\n  if (!Number.isInteger(minutes) || minutes <= 0) {\n    console.error(\"--minutes must be a whole number of minutes\");\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n\n  const atText = arg(\"at\");\n  let pinnedStartAt: string | null = null;\n  if (atText) {\n    const pinned = hostWallClockToUtc(tz, atText);\n    if (!pinned) {\n      console.error(`--at must be \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm\" (in ${tz}) or a full ISO timestamp, got \"${atText}\"`);\n      process.exit(2);\n    }\n    if (pinned.getTime() <= Date.now()) {\n      console.error(`--at is in the past: ${inHostTz(tz, pinned.toISOString())}`);\n      process.exit(2);\n    }\n    pinnedStartAt = pinned.toISOString();\n  }\n\n  // A pinned link has already answered the notice question, so the column only\n  // matters for a picker link. Without --at, the default stays the ordinary 24\n  // hours rather than silently loosening every link that omits the flag.\n  const notice = Number(arg(\"notice\") ?? (pinnedStartAt ? 0 : 1440));\n  const window = Number(arg(\"window\") ?? 6);\n  if (!Number.isInteger(notice) || notice < 0 || !Number.isInteger(window) || window <= 0) {\n    console.error(\"--notice must be >= 0 minutes and --window a positive number of business days\");\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n\n  const slug = slugFor(forName);\n  const row = {\n    slug,\n    label: arg(\"label\") ?? \"LSAT Session\",\n    length_minutes: minutes,\n    slot_interval_minutes: null,\n    minimum_notice_minutes: notice,\n    buffer_before_minutes: 0,\n    buffer_after_minutes: 0,\n    window_business_days: window,\n    price_cents: priceCents,\n    currency: \"usd\",\n    requires_payment: true,\n    active: true,\n    single_use: true,\n    pinned_start_at: pinnedStartAt,\n    note: arg(\"note\") ?? `One-off link for ${forName}.`,\n  };\n\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\"booking_event_types\").insert(row);\n  if (error) throw error;\n\n  // Ask the booking engine what this link can sell. A pinned link that comes\n  // back empty clashes with something already on the calendar, and a link that\n  // would fail the moment it was opened is worse than no link -- so it is\n  // deleted rather than handed over. Nothing has seen the URL yet.\n  let slots: { start_at: string }[];\n  try {\n    slots = await slotsFor(slug);\n  } catch (problem) {\n    await supabase.from(\"booking_event_types\").delete().eq(\"slug\", slug);\n    throw problem;\n  }\n\n  if (!slots.length) {\n    await supabase.from(\"booking_event_types\").delete().eq(\"slug\", slug);\n    console.error(\n      pinnedStartAt\n        ? `booking-link: ${inHostTz(tz, pinnedStartAt)} clashes with something already on your calendar. No link was created.`\n        : \"booking-link: this link would have no open times at all (check your weekly hours and window). No link was created.\",\n    );\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  const url = `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/x/${slug}`;\n  console.log(`\\n  ${url}\\n`);\n  console.log(`  ${forName} · $${(priceCents / 100).toFixed(2)} · ${minutes} minutes`);\n  console.log(\n    pinnedStartAt\n      ? `  Books ${inHostTz(tz, pinnedStartAt)} and only that time.`\n      : `  ${slots.length} open time${slots.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}, first ${inHostTz(tz, slots[0].start_at)}` +\n          `${notice === 0 ? \" (no minimum notice)\" : ` (${notice} minutes' notice)`}.`,\n  );\n  console.log(\"  Sells one session, then closes.\\n\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------- main\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  await signIn();\n  const tz = await hostTimezone();\n  const revokeSlug = arg(\"revoke\");\n  if (has(\"list\")) return list(tz);\n  if (revokeSlug) return revoke(revokeSlug);\n  return create(tz);\n}\n\nmain()\n  .then(() => process.exit(0))\n  .catch((problem) => {\n    console.error(`booking-link: ${problem instanceof Error ? problem.message : String(problem)}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n"}

import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter } from "react-router-dom";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import SingleSession, { type SingleSessionApi } from "./SingleSession";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";

const bookingTheme = readFileSync("src/pages/booking/booking-theme.css", "utf8");

const slot = { start_at: "2026-08-18T17:00:00.000Z", end_at: "2026-08-18T18:10:00.000Z" };

function api(overrides: Partial<SingleSessionApi> = {}): SingleSessionApi {
  return {
    info: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ host: { name: "Joshua", title: "LSAT Session" }, duration_minutes: 70, amount_cents: 4500, currency: "usd" }),
    slots: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ slots: [slot] }),
    hold: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ checkout_url: "https://checkout.stripe.test/session", booking_token: "book-token", checkout_session_id: "cs_test" }),
    status: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ status: "checkout_open", start_at: slot.start_at, end_at: slot.end_at }),
    abandon: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ status: "abandoned" }),
    cancel: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ status: "cancelled", start_at: slot.start_at, end_at: slot.end_at }),
    getTurnstileToken: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue("turnstile-token"),
    redirectToCheckout: vi.fn(),
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function renderPage(client = api(), entry = "/book/session", event = "single-session") {
  render(<MemoryRouter initialEntries={[entry]}><SingleSession api={client} event={event} /></MemoryRouter>);
  return client;
}

async function chooseSlotAndFillForm() {
  await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Tuesday, August 18/i });
  fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByRole("button", { name: /1:00 PM/i })[0]);
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Your name/i), { target: { value: "Booker Name" } });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Email address/i), { target: { value: "BOOKER@example.com" } });
}

describe("SingleSession", () => {
  beforeEach(() => sessionStorage.clear());

  it("reuses one idempotency key and one Hold when Pay to book is double-submitted", async () => {
    const client = renderPage();
    await chooseSlotAndFillForm();
    const submit = screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Pay to book/i });
    fireEvent.click(submit);
    fireEvent.click(submit);

    await waitFor(() => expect(client.hold).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(client.hold).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({
      start_at: slot.start_at,
      email: "booker@example.com",
      turnstile_token: "turnstile-token",
      client_idempotency_key: expect.any(String),
    }));
    expect(client.redirectToCheckout).toHaveBeenCalledWith("https://checkout.stripe.test/session");
  });

  it("confirms a free consultation without sending anyone to checkout", async () => {
    // The free path finishes inside hold: no checkout_url comes back, and the
    // booking is already booked. Redirecting here would land on a Stripe page
    // that was never created.
    const client = api({
      info: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        host: { name: "Joshua", title: "Consultation" },
        duration_minutes: 30, amount_cents: 0, currency: "usd", requires_payment: false,
      }),
      hold: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        checkout_url: null,
        checkout_session_id: null,
        booking_token: "book-token",
        booking: { status: "booked", start_at: slot.start_at, end_at: slot.end_at },
      }),
    });
    renderPage(client, "/book/consultation", "consultation");

    await screen.findAllByText("Free");
    await chooseSlotAndFillForm();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Confirm booking/i }));

    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /You're booked/i });
    expect(client.redirectToCheckout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

async function invoke<T>(action: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("single-session", { body: { action, ...extra } });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  const result = data as { error?: string } & T;
  if (result?.error) throw new Error(result.error);
  return result;
}

## assistant

Done. Here's what exists now.

**A one-off link is a `booking_event_types` row.** That table already defines the ordinary session and the consultation, and every layer below it — page, edge function, slot policy, claim transaction — is already keyed by event slug. So a one-off link books through exactly the code a normal session books through. No parallel booking path.

```bash
npm run booking:link -- --for "Dakota" --price 60 --at "2026-09-10 18:00"
```

Prints `https://lsatprep.study/book/x/dakota-x7k2m9qp4rtv`. Also `--minutes`, `--notice`, `--window`, `--label`, `--note`, plus `--list` and `--revoke <slug>`.

**Short notice and conflicts.** `--at` pins one exact time: the link offers only that time and the notice, rolling-window and weekly-hours rules don't apply, because choosing the time *is* that decision. Without `--at` you get a normal picker with per-link knobs — `--notice 0` means "book me in an hour". Double-booking is never bypassed: it's still refused inside the booking transaction.

Clash checking at mint time works by asking the booking engine itself — the script creates the row, calls the `slots` action, and deletes the row if it comes back empty. That's deliberate: live Google free/busy isn't reachable from a script, so reimplementing the check would have been a second, wrong answer.

**Single use** is enforced by a trigger under a per-slug advisory lock, so two people clicking the same link at the same moment can't both get through. A hold holds the link; expiring or abandoning it releases it; a cancelled or refunded booking stays spent.

**Two bugs found on the way.** `single_session_bookings` still carried `end_at = start_at + 70 minutes` and `amount_cents = 4500` CHECKs from when there was one sellable thing. Those blocked custom prices — and they also mean the seeded 30-minute `consultation` event cannot currently be booked at all; its insert violates the 70-minute check. Both are replaced by a trigger reading the event's own length. Separately, the page showed a hardcoded `1h 10m` and `$45.00` while an event was unknown, which for a one-off link would misstate the price; both now read from the event or show a dash.

**Verified:** typecheck clean; `bookingSlots` + `bookingCalendar` specs pass (14). Route mounts and rejects an unknown slug in the dev browser (screenshot above). The 4 failing `SingleSession` specs are pre-existing — I ran them against the unmodified file and got the identical 4 failures; they're machine-timezone dependent, not from this change.

**Not verified, and it can't be from here:** the actual booking of a one-off link. The migration and the edge function both need Lovable to apply and deploy them, and the deployed function is what the dev app calls. Deploy order is safe either way — the function only calls the new RPC for events with `single_use` set, which no existing row has.

**I did not commit.** `src/App.tsx` has in-flight work that isn't mine (an `ActivityLink` route), and committing the file would sweep it in. Say the word and I'll commit either just my files or the lot.
